- **aes-author-response**: 在为《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 撰写审稿意见答复与修回说明时调用。适用于收到编辑部"修改后再审/退修"通知后，逐条回应外审专家意见、撰写"修改说明"对照表、在正文中标注修改痕迹、处理相互矛盾或要求补做实验的意见、把握修回时限与再审轮次。帮助电子/信息/通信/信号处理方向的中文稿件在《电子学报》多级审理中稳步走向录用，避免因答复不规范或情绪化措辞被再次退修甚至退稿。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-author-response) · ★1.1k)

- **aes-review-process**: 当你需要理解《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 审稿流程、稿件状态与时限时使用。覆盖投稿受理→编辑初审→双盲外审(同行评议)→复审→主编/编委会终审→退修与再审轮次→录用的多级审理链路，各阶段可能的决定(录用/退修/退稿)、6 个月未答复红线、如何解读稿件状态、如何礼貌催稿、以及本刊与英文姊妹刊 Chinese Journal of Electronics 的分流关系。帮助电子/信息/通信方向作者对《电子学报》的审理节奏与决策点建立准确预期，合理规划投稿与修回。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-review-process) · ★1.1k)
- **aes-submission**: 在向《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 正式提交稿件前做投稿就绪审计时调用。覆盖官网 ejournal.org.cn 在线投稿系统、注册与稿件同步、官方 Word/LaTeX 模板、中英文摘要与关键词、中图分类号(TN 为主)、GB/T 7714 参考文献、双盲评审的匿名化要求、稿件评审费(中文约 199 元/英文约 260 元 待核实以系统为准)、涉密脱敏、栏目归属与稿件类型选择，并在上传前做退稿风险分诊。适用于把一份电子/信息/通信/信号处理方向的中文原创论文打磨到符合本刊四级审理门槛的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **aes-supplementary**: 当你为《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 稿件组织附录与补充材料时使用。覆盖哪些内容应放正文、哪些应下沉到附录/在线补充材料、多媒体附件(音视频/信号波形/大数据表)的提交方式、公式推导与完整证明的呈现、扩展实验与超参数表、双盲阶段补充材料的匿名化、以及与正文的引用对应关系。帮助电子/信息/信号处理方向作者在《电子学报》有限正文篇幅内保持主线清晰，同时用补充材料完整承载可复现细节与扩展证据。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-supplementary) · ★1.1k)
- **aes-topic-selection**: 当你为《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 判断选题是否对口、定位到合适栏目/学科方向时使用。覆盖本刊电子科学与技术、信息与通信工程、信号与信息处理、电路与系统、电磁场与微波、集成电路等学科侧重，如何评估选题的创新性与本刊契合度、区别于计算机类与控制类兄弟刊、判断投中文《电子学报》还是英文姊妹刊 Chinese Journal of Electronics、以及原创论文与综述的定位差异。帮助作者在动笔前就把选题放进《电子学报》的正确坐标，避免因不对口在初审被退。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **aes-workflow**: 当你需要《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 投稿全流程总览与阶段调度时使用。把选题、写作、投稿、双盲外审、多级审理、修回、录用、校样到出版串成一条端到端时间线，指明每个阶段该调用哪个 aes-* 技能、关键决策点、6 个月答复红线、与英文姊妹刊 Chinese Journal of Electronics 的分流时机、以及常见时间陷阱。适合电子/信息/通信方向作者在动笔前或投稿中做整体规划，避免顺序错乱、错过时限或在错误阶段做错误的事。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **aes-writing-style**: 当你按《电子学报》(Acta Electronica Sinica, AES) 规范打磨中文科技论文写作时使用。覆盖中英文题名、结构式中文摘要与英文 Abstract、中英文关键词、中图分类号(TN 大类)、量和单位与法定计量、公式与变量排版、图表规范、GB/T 7714 顺序编码制参考文献中英文著录、以及电子/信息/信号处理领域术语与数学表达的一致性。帮助作者写出符合《电子学报》体例、语言严谨、可被外审专家顺畅评阅的中文旗舰学报稿件，避免因写作不规范在初审或外审被扣分。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Acta-Electronica-Sinica-Skills/skills/aes-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
- **aes256gcm-machineid-credential-store**: Cross-platform encrypted credential file (AES-256-GCM) keyed with a PBKDF2-derived key whose salt is pulled from a hardware-stable OS identifier so the store survives hostname/DHCP changes. ([kjuhwa/skills-hub](https://github.com/kjuhwa/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/security/aes256gcm-machineid-credential-store))
- **aesop-cry-wolf**: Use when about to emit a warning, flag, or alert for a LOW-confidence issue — the alert-fatigue / signal-credibility discipline. False alarms have an asymmetric cost: after repeated low-confidence warnings, the operator stops reading the channel, and the one true warning gets ignored. Fires on choosing a log level (warn vs info vs error), inflating a PR title/commit to "CRITICAL", marking speculative concerns as ⚠️ risks, or surfacing so many maybe-problems in a review that the real one drowns. Anti-pattern handle for defensive over-flagging. ([evnchn-agentic/aesop-skills](https://github.com/evnchn-agentic/aesop-skills/tree/HEAD/aesop-cry-wolf))
- **aesop-golden-goose**: Use when about to extract maximum present value from a RENEWABLE source in a way that degrades the source itself — the kill-the-source / unsustainable-extraction anti-pattern. Fires when about to max out an API rate limit or quota to ship one task faster (risks the access), flood the operator with low-priority pings (burns attention/trust budget), sacrifice maintainability for one task's throughput (poisons the well for all future work on that code), or over-extract from any shared resource you'll need again. The schema: short-term win, long-term loss, because you destroyed the thing that pro… ([evnchn-agentic/aesop-skills](https://github.com/evnchn-agentic/aesop-skills/tree/HEAD/aesop-golden-goose))
- **aesop-sour-grapes**: Use when, just after abandoning or failing at something, you catch yourself concluding "that wasn't worth reaching anyway" — the post-mortem self-deception / motivated-reasoning anti-pattern. Fires when writing a post-mortem after a failure or rollback, recording a lesson after a retreat, or when an operator/agent's stated reason for quitting smells like comfort rather than analysis. The tell: a goal re-rated as unworthy ONLY because it turned out to be unreachable — a re-rating that would have been absent had the attempt succeeded. ([evnchn-agentic/aesop-skills](https://github.com/evnchn-agentic/aesop-skills/tree/HEAD/aesop-sour-grapes))
- **aesop-voice-pipeline**: Real-time on-device voice engine (mic -&gt; VAD -&gt; STT -&gt; TTS -&gt; speaker) for this Moto Razr, plus NPU/STT benchmark tools recovered from ~/downloads. Use for anything involving live voice interaction, Gemma-on-NPU benchmarking, or whisper.cpp/Parakeet testing. - **affordance-audit**: Audit an API, CLI, or config surface for what it makes easy (affordances) and what it makes easy to get wrong (anti-affordances, footguns).
